class op4_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 8, p1: 768}
    index0 = 768*p0 + p1
    index1 = p0
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf0', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf1', get_index_1)
        sub = ops.sub(load, load_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_2)
        constant = ops.constant(768.0, torch.float32)
        truediv = ops.truediv(load_2, constant)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1e-12, torch.float32)
        add = ops.add(truediv, constant_1)
        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add)
        mul = ops.mul(sub, rsqrt)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf4', get_index_3, mul, None)
        return store

class op5_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 8, p1: 8}
    index0 = p1
    index1 = 8*p0 + p1
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('primals_9', get_index)
        to_dtype = ops.to_dtype(load, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.int64)
        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        sub = ops.sub(constant, to_dtype)
        to_dtype_1 = ops.to_dtype(sub, torch.bool, src_dtype = torch.float32)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_1 = ops.load('primals_9', get_index_1)
        to_dtype_2 = ops.to_dtype(load_1, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        sub_1 = ops.sub(constant_1, to_dtype_2)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(-3.4028234663852886e+38, torch.float32)
        where = ops.where(to_dtype_1, constant_2, sub_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        store = ops.store('buf5', get_index_2, where, None)
        return store

class op45_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 8, p1: 768}
    index0 = 768*p0 + p1
    index1 = p0
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf41', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf38', get_index_1)
        add = ops.add(load, load_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf42', get_index_2)
        sub = ops.sub(add, load_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('buf43', get_index_3)
        constant = ops.constant(768.0, torch.float32)
        truediv = ops.truediv(load_3, constant)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1e-12, torch.float32)
        add_1 = ops.add(truediv, constant_1)
        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add_1)
        mul = ops.mul(sub, rsqrt)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf45', get_index_4, mul, None)
        return store

class op105_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 8, p1: 768}
    index0 = 768*p0 + p1
    index1 = p0
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf101', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf98', get_index_1)
        add = ops.add(load, load_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf102', get_index_2)
        sub = ops.sub(add, load_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('buf103', get_index_3)
        constant = ops.constant(768.0, torch.float32)
        truediv = ops.truediv(load_3, constant)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1e-12, torch.float32)
        add_1 = ops.add(truediv, constant_1)
        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add_1)
        mul = ops.mul(sub, rsqrt)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf105', get_index_4, mul, None)
        return store

class op165_loop_body:

        load = ops.load('buf161', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf158', get_index_1)
        add = ops.add(load, load_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf162', get_index_2)
        sub = ops.sub(add, load_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('buf163', get_index_3)
        constant = ops.constant(768.0, torch.float32)
        truediv = ops.truediv(load_3, constant)

        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add_1)
        mul = ops.mul(sub, rsqrt)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf165', get_index_4, mul, None)
        return store

class op225_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 8, p1: 768}
    index0 = 768*p0 + p1
    index1 = p0
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf221', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf218', get_index_1)
        add = ops.add(load, load_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf222', get_index_2)
        sub = ops.sub(add, load_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('buf223', get_index_3)
        constant = ops.constant(768.0, torch.float32)
        truediv = ops.truediv(load_3, constant)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1e-12, torch.float32)
        add_1 = ops.add(truediv, constant_1)
        rsqrt = ops.rsqrt(add_1)
        mul = ops.mul(sub, rsqrt)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf225', get_index_4, mul, None)
        return store
